   00037: function ymdToNumber(ymd) {
   00038:   if (!ymd) return NaN;
   00039:   const m = String(ymd).match(/^(\d{4})-(\d{2})-(\d{2})$/);
   00040:   if (!m) return NaN;
   00041:   return Number(`${m[1]}${m[2]}${m[3]}`);
   00042: }
   00043: 
   00044: function dayDiffInclusiveUTC(ymdFrom, ymdTo) {
   00045:   const toUTC = (ymd) => {
   00046:     const m = String(ymd).match(/^(\d{4})-(\d{2})-(\d{2})$/);
   00047:     if (!m) return null;
   00048:     return new Date(Date.UTC(Number(m[1]), Number(m[2]) - 1, Number(m[3])));
   00049:   };
   00050: 
   00051:   const da = toUTC(ymdFrom);
   00052:   const db = toUTC(ymdTo);
   00053:   if (!da || !db) return NaN;
   00054: 
   00055:   const ms = db.getTime() - da.getTime();
   00056:   if (!Number.isFinite(ms)) return NaN;
   00057: 
   00058:   return Math.floor(ms / 86400000) + 1;
   00059: }

   00074: function normHHMM(value) {
   00075:   const s = String(value || "").trim();
   00076:   if (!s) return "";
   00077: 
   00078:   const mh = s.match(/^(\d{1,2})h$/i);
   00079:   if (mh) return `${String(mh[1]).padStart(2, "0")}:00`;
   00080: 
   00081:   const mISO = s.match(/^(\d{1,2}):(\d{1,2})(?::(\d{1,2}))?$/);
   00082:   if (mISO) {
   00083:     const hh = String(mISO[1]).padStart(2, "0");
   00084:     const mm = String(mISO[2]).padStart(2, "0");
   00085:     return `${hh}:${mm}`;
   00086:   }
   00087: 
   00088:   const m2 = s.match(/^(\d{1,2})$/);
   00089:   if (m2) return `${String(m2[1]).padStart(2, "0")}:00`;
   00090: 
   00091:   return s;
   00092: }

   00257: async function mapWithConcurrency(
   00258:   items,
   00259:   concurrency,
   00260:   worker
   00261: ) {
   00262:   const output = new Array(items.length);
   00263:   let cursor = 0;
   00264: 
   00265:   async function run() {
   00266:     while (true) {
   00267:       const index = cursor;
   00268:       cursor += 1;
   00269: 
   00270:       if (index >= items.length) {
   00271:         return;
   00272:       }
   00273: 
   00274:       output[index] = await worker(
   00275:         items[index],
   00276:         index
   00277:       );
   00278:     }
   00279:   }
   00280: 
>> 00281:   const runners = Array.from(
   00282:     {
   00283:       length: Math.max(
   00284:         1,
   00285:         Math.min(concurrency, items.length)
   00286:       ),
   00287:     },
   00288:     run
   00289:   );
   00290: 
   00291:   await Promise.all(runners);
   00292: 
   00293:   return output;
   00294: }

   00383: function formatScore(value) {

   00385: 
   00386:   if (!Number.isFinite(number)) {
   00387:     return "0,000";
   00388:   }
   00389: 
   00390:   return number.toLocaleString("pt-BR", {
   00391:     minimumFractionDigits: 3,
   00392:     maximumFractionDigits: 3,
   00393:   });
   00394: }

   00396: function formatPercent(value) {
   00397:   const number = Number(value);
   00398: 
   00399:   if (!Number.isFinite(number)) {
   00400:     return "0,00%";
   00401:   }

   00403:   return (number * 100).toLocaleString(
   00404:     "pt-BR",
   00405:     {
   00406:       minimumFractionDigits: 2,
   00407:       maximumFractionDigits: 2,
   00408:     }
   00409:   ) + "%";
   00410: }

   00424: export async function mapWithConcurrency(items, limitN, mapper) {
   00425:   const arr = Array.isArray(items) ? items : [];
   00426:   const concurrency = Math.max(1, Number(limitN) || 3);
   00427:   const results = new Array(arr.length);
   00428: 
   00429:   let idx = 0;
   00430: 
   00431:   async function worker() {
   00432:     // ✅ claim seguro do próximo índice
   00433:     // (evita disputa em cenários com yield/microtasks)
   00434:     // eslint-disable-next-line no-constant-condition
   00435:     while (true) {
   00436:       const current = idx;
   00437:       idx += 1;
   00438:       if (current >= arr.length) break;
   00439:       results[current] = await mapper(arr[current], current);
   00440:     }
   00441:   }
   00442: 
   00443:   await Promise.all(
>> 00444:     Array.from({ length: Math.min(concurrency, arr.length) }, () => worker())
   00445:   );
   00446: 
   00447:   return results;
   00448: }

   00084: function normalizeRatio(value, maxValue) {
   00085:   const n = Number(value) || 0;
   00086:   const max = Number(maxValue) || 0;
   00087: 
   00088:   if (max <= 0) return 0;
   00089:   return Math.max(0, Math.min(1, n / max));
   00090: }

   00092: function normalizeWeights(customWeights = {}) {
   00093:   const merged = {
   00094:     ...DEFAULT_WEIGHTS,
   00095:     ...(customWeights && typeof customWeights === "object"
   00096:       ? customWeights
   00097:       : {}),
   00098:   };
   00099: 
   00100:   const keys = Object.keys(DEFAULT_WEIGHTS);
   00101:   const total = keys.reduce(
   00102:     (sum, key) => sum + Math.max(0, Number(merged[key]) || 0),
   00103:     0
   00104:   );
   00105: 
   00106:   if (total <= 0) return { ...DEFAULT_WEIGHTS };
   00107: 
   00108:   return keys.reduce((out, key) => {
   00109:     out[key] = Math.max(0, Number(merged[key]) || 0) / total;
   00110:     return out;
   00111:   }, {});
   00112: }

   00490: function normalizeLoteriaKey(v) {

   00527: function getTodayYmdSafe() {

   00538: function yearRangeToDates(minY, maxY, floorISO, ceilISO) {
   00539:   const y1 = Number(minY);
   00540:   const y2 = Number(maxY);
   00541:   if (!Number.isFinite(y1) || !Number.isFinite(y2)) return null;
   00542: 
   00543:   const a = Math.min(y1, y2);
   00544:   const b = Math.max(y1, y2);
   00545: 
   00546:   let from = `${a}-01-01`;
   00547:   let to = `${b}-12-31`;
   00548: 
   00549:   if (isISODate(floorISO) && from < floorISO) from = floorISO;
   00550:   if (isISODate(ceilISO) && to > ceilISO) to = ceilISO;
   00551: 
   00552:   if (isISODate(from) && isISODate(to) && from > to) to = from;
   00553:   return { from, to };
   00554: }

   00030: 
   00031: function pad2(n) {
   00032:   return String(n).padStart(2, "0");
   00033: }
   00034: 
   00035: function isISODate(s) {
   00036:   return /^(\d{4})-(\d{2})-(\d{2})$/.test(String(s || ""));
   00037: }
   00038: 
   00039: function formatBR(iso) {
   00040:   if (!isISODate(iso)) return "";
   00041:   const [y, m, d] = iso.split("-");
   00042:   return `${d}/${m}/${y}`;
   00043: }
   00044: 
   00045: function toUTCDate(iso) {
   00046:   if (!isISODate(iso)) return null;
   00047:   const [y, m, d] = iso.split("-").map(Number);
   00048:   if (!y || !m || !d) return null;
   00049:   return new Date(Date.UTC(y, m - 1, d));
   00050: }
   00051: 
   00052: function fromUTCDate(dt) {
   00053:   if (!(dt instanceof Date) || !Number.isFinite(dt.getTime())) return null;
   00054:   const y = dt.getUTCFullYear();
   00055:   const m = pad2(dt.getUTCMonth() + 1);
   00056:   const d = pad2(dt.getUTCDate());
   00057:   return `${y}-${m}-${d}`;
   00058: }
   00059: 
   00060: function clamp(n, a, b) {
   00061:   return Math.max(a, Math.min(b, n));
   00062: }
   00063: 
   00064: function clampDateISO(iso, minISO, maxISO) {
   00065:   const dt = toUTCDate(iso);
   00066:   const minDt = toUTCDate(minISO);
   00067:   const maxDt = toUTCDate(maxISO);
   00068: 
   00069:   if (!minDt || !maxDt) return isISODate(iso) ? iso : null;
   00070:   if (!dt) return maxISO;
   00071: 
   00072:   const clamped = new Date(clamp(dt.getTime(), minDt.getTime(), maxDt.getTime()));
   00073:   return fromUTCDate(clamped) || maxISO;
   00074: }
   00075: 
   00076: function daysBetweenUTC(aISO, bISO) {
   00077:   const a = toUTCDate(aISO);
   00078:   const b = toUTCDate(bISO);
   00079:   if (!a || !b) return 0;
   00080:   const ms = b.getTime() - a.getTime();
   00081:   if (!Number.isFinite(ms)) return 0;
   00082:   return Math.floor(ms / 86400000);
   00083: }
   00084: 
   00085: function addDaysUTC(iso, days) {
   00086:   const dt = toUTCDate(iso);
   00087:   if (!dt) return iso;
   00088:   dt.setUTCDate(dt.getUTCDate() + Number(days || 0));
   00089:   return fromUTCDate(dt);
   00090: }

   00087: function buildImgCandidates(getImgFromGrupo, grupo) {
   00088:   const base96 = safeStr(getImgFromGrupo(grupo, 96));
   00089:   const base128 = safeStr(getImgFromGrupo(grupo, 128));
   00090:   const base64 = safeStr(getImgFromGrupo(grupo, 64));

   00096:     out.push(src);
   00097: 
   00098:     if (/\.png(\?|#|$)/i.test(src)) {
   00099:       out.push(src.replace(/\.png(\?|#|$)/i, ".jpg$1"));
   00100:       out.push(src.replace(/\.png(\?|#|$)/i, ".jpeg$1"));
   00101:     }
   00102: 
   00103:     if (/\.jpe?g(\?|#|$)/i.test(src)) {
   00104:       out.push(src.replace(/\.jpe?g(\?|#|$)/i, ".png$1"));
   00105:     }
   00106:   }
   00107: 
   00108:   return uniq(out);
   00109: }

   00122: function makeImgVariants(src) {
   00123:   const current = normalizeImgSrc(src);
   00124:   if (!current) return [];
   00125: 
   00126:   const baseNoQuery = current.split("?")[0];
   00127:   const out = [];

   00133:   out.push(...ensureSizeVariants(baseNoQuery));
   00134: 
   00135:   // alterna case/extensão
   00136:   if (baseNoQuery.match(/\.png$/)) out.push(baseNoQuery.replace(/\.png$/, ".PNG"));
   00137:   if (baseNoQuery.match(/\.PNG$/)) out.push(baseNoQuery.replace(/\.PNG$/, ".png"));
   00138: 
   00139:   // tenta jpg/jpeg
   00140:   out.push(baseNoQuery.replace(/\.(png|PNG)$/i, ".jpg"));
   00141:   out.push(baseNoQuery.replace(/\.(png|PNG|jpg)$/i, ".jpeg"));
   00142: 
   00143:   // também troca extensão nas variantes de size (caso stem exista só em jpg etc)
   00144:   const extra = [];
   00145:   for (const p of out) {
   00146:     extra.push(p.replace(/\.(png|PNG)$/i, ".jpg"));
   00147:     extra.push(p.replace(/\.(png|PNG|jpg)$/i, ".jpeg"));
   00148:     if (p.match(/\.png$/)) extra.push(p.replace(/\.png$/, ".PNG"));
   00149:     if (p.match(/\.PNG$/)) extra.push(p.replace(/\.PNG$/, ".png"));
   00150:   }
   00151: 
>> 00152:   return Array.from(new Set([...out, ...extra].filter(Boolean)));
   00153: }

   00155: function RowImg({ variants, alt, fallbackText }) {
   00156:   const [failed, setFailed] = useState(false);
   00157: 
   00158:   const primary = variants?.[0] || "";
   00159: 
   00160:   // ✅ quando variants muda (nova linha/grupo), reseta o fallback
   00161:   useEffect(() => {
   00162:     setFailed(false);
   00163:   }, [primary]); // muda o "principal" => reseta
   00164: 
   00165:   if (!Array.isArray(variants) || !variants.length || failed) {
   00166:     return <div className="imgFallback">{fallbackText || "—"}</div>;
   00167:   }
   00168: 
   00169:   return (
   00170:     <img
   00171:       key={variants[0]} // ✅ força reset do <img> ao trocar a imagem base
   00172:       src={variants[0]}
   00173:       alt={alt}
   00174:       loading="lazy"
   00175:       data-try="0"
   00176:       onError={(e) => {
   00177:         const imgEl = e.currentTarget;
   00178:         const i = Number(imgEl.dataset.try || "0");
   00179:         const next = variants[i + 1];
   00180: 
   00181:         if (next) {
   00182:           imgEl.dataset.try = String(i + 1);
   00183:           imgEl.src = next;
   00184:         } else {
   00185:           setFailed(true);
   00186:         }
   00187:       }}
   00188:     />
   00189:   );
   00190: }

   00189: function normalizeUfToQueryKey(input) {
   00190:   const s = safeStr(input).toUpperCase();
   00191:   if (!s) return "";
   00192:   if (s.includes("_") || s.length > 2) return s;
   00193:   return UF_TO_LOTTERY_KEY[s] || s;
   00194: }
   00195: 
   00196: function lotteryLabelFromKey(key) {
   00197:   const s = safeStr(key).toUpperCase();
   00198:   if (s === "PT_RIO") return "RIO";
   00199:   if (s === "FEDERAL") return "FEDERAL";
   00200:   if (s.length === 2) return s;
   00201:   const parts = s.split("_");
   00202:   return parts[parts.length - 1] || s;
   00203: }

>> 00209: function clampYmd(ymd, minDate, maxDate) {
   00210:   const d = normalizeToYMD(ymd);
   00211:   if (!isYMD(d)) return null;
   00212: 
   00213:   let out = d;
   00214: 
>> 00215:   if (isYMD(minDate) && out < minDate) out = minDate;
>> 00216:   if (isYMD(maxDate) && out > maxDate) out = maxDate;
   00217: 
   00218:   return out;
   00219: }

>> 00221: function normalizeRangeWithBounds(fromIn, toIn, minDate, maxDate) {
>> 00222:   let from = clampYmd(fromIn, minDate, maxDate);
>> 00223:   let to = clampYmd(toIn, minDate, maxDate);
   00224: 
>> 00225:   const fallback = clampYmd(todayYMDLocal(), minDate, maxDate) || maxDate || minDate || todayYMDLocal();
   00226: 
   00227:   if (!from) from = fallback;
   00228:   if (!to) to = from;
   00229: 
   00230:   if (from > to) [from, to] = [to, from];
   00231: 
   00232:   return { from, to };
   00233: }

   00239: function downloadBlob(filename, blob) {
   00240:   const url = URL.createObjectURL(blob);
   00241:   const a = document.createElement("a");
   00242:   a.href = url;
   00243:   a.download = filename;
   00244:   document.body.appendChild(a);
   00245:   a.click();
   00246:   a.remove();
   00247:   URL.revokeObjectURL(url);
   00248: }

   00250: async function exportExcelXlsx({
   00251:   filename,
   00252:   title,
   00253:   metaLines,
   00254:   columns,
   00255:   rows,
   00256:   textCols,
   00257:   sheetName = "resultados",
   00258: }) {
   00259:   const mod = await import("xlsx");
   00260:   const XLSX = mod?.default ?? mod;
   00261: 
   00262:   const aoa = [];
   00263:   aoa.push([String(title || "Exportação")]);
   00264:   (metaLines || []).forEach((l) => aoa.push([String(l || "")]));
   00265:   aoa.push([]);
   00266:   aoa.push(columns.map((c) => String(c ?? "")));
   00267:   rows.forEach((r) => aoa.push((r || []).map((v) => String(v ?? ""))));
   00268: 
   00269:   const ws = XLSX.utils.aoa_to_sheet(aoa);
   00270: 
   00271:   // linhas acima dos dados:
   00272:   // title(1) + meta(n) + blank(1) + header(1) => firstDataRowAoa = n + 3 (0-based)
   00273:   const headerRowNumber = 1 + (metaLines?.length || 0) + 1 + 1;
   00274:   ws["!freeze"] = { xSplit: 0, ySplit: headerRowNumber };
   00275: 
   00276:   const firstDataRowAoa = (metaLines?.length || 0) + 3;
   00277:   const tcols = Array.isArray(textCols) ? textCols : [];
   00278: 
   00279:   if (tcols.length) {
   00280:     for (let r = 0; r < rows.length; r += 1) {
   00281:       const aoaRowIndex = firstDataRowAoa + r;
   00282:       for (const c of tcols) {
   00283:         const cellAddr = XLSX.utils.encode_cell({ r: aoaRowIndex, c });
   00284:         const cell = ws[cellAddr];
   00285:         if (!cell) continue;
   00286:         cell.t = "s";
   00287:         cell.z = "@";
   00288:         cell.v = String(cell.v ?? "");
   00289:       }
   00290:     }
   00291:   }
   00292: 
   00293:   const wb = XLSX.utils.book_new();
   00294:   XLSX.utils.book_append_sheet(wb, ws, sheetName);
   00295: 
   00296:   const array = XLSX.write(wb, { bookType: "xlsx", type: "array" });
   00297: 
   00298:   const blob = new Blob([array], {
   00299:     type: "application/vnd.openxmlformats-officedocument.spreadsheetml.sheet",
   00300:   });
   00301: 
   00302:   downloadBlob(filename, blob);
   00303: }

   00244: function clampYmd(ymd, minYmd, maxYmd) {
   00245:   const d = safeStr(ymd);
   00246:   if (!isYMD(d)) return null;
   00247: 
   00248:   let out = d;
   00249:   const min = safeStr(minYmd);
   00250:   const max = safeStr(maxYmd);
   00251: 
   00252:   if (isYMD(min) && out < min) out = min;
   00253:   if (isYMD(max) && out > max) out = max;
   00254: 
   00255:   return out;
   00256: }
   00257: 
   00258: function normalizeSingleDateWithBounds(dateIn, minYmd, maxYmd) {
   00259:   const d = clampYmd(dateIn, minYmd, maxYmd);
   00260:   if (d) return d;
   00261: 
   00262:   const fallback =
   00263:     clampYmd(todayYMDLocal(), minYmd, maxYmd) ||
   00264:     (isYMD(maxYmd) ? maxYmd : null) ||
   00265:     (isYMD(minYmd) ? minYmd : null) ||
   00266:     todayYMDLocal();
   00267: 
   00268:   return fallback;
   00269: }

   00066: function startOfMonthYMD(ymd) {
   00067:   const d = ymdToDateLocal(ymd);
   00068:   if (!d) return todayYMDLocal().slice(0, 8) + "01";
   00069:   d.setDate(1);
   00070:   return dateToYMDLocal(d);
   00071: }
   00072: 
   00073: function endOfMonthYMD(ymd) {
   00074:   const d = ymdToDateLocal(ymd);
   00075:   if (!d) return todayYMDLocal();
   00076:   d.setMonth(d.getMonth() + 1, 0);
   00077:   return dateToYMDLocal(d);
   00078: }
   00079: 
   00080: function shiftMonthYMD(ymd, deltaMonths) {
   00081:   const d = ymdToDateLocal(startOfMonthYMD(ymd));
   00082:   if (!d) return startOfMonthYMD(todayYMDLocal());
   00083:   d.setMonth(d.getMonth() + Number(deltaMonths || 0), 1);
   00084:   return dateToYMDLocal(d);
   00085: }
   00086: 
   00087: function monthTitleBR(ymd) {
   00088:   const d = ymdToDateLocal(startOfMonthYMD(ymd));
   00089:   if (!d) return "";
   00090:   const s = d.toLocaleDateString("pt-BR", { month: "long", year: "numeric" });
   00091:   return s.charAt(0).toUpperCase() + s.slice(1);
   00092: }
   00093: 
   00094: function buildCalendarCells(monthYmd) {
   00095:   const first = ymdToDateLocal(startOfMonthYMD(monthYmd));
   00096:   if (!first) return [];
   00097: 
   00098:   const start = new Date(first);
   00099:   start.setDate(first.getDate() - first.getDay());
   00100: 
>> 00101:   return Array.from({ length: 42 }, (_, idx) => {
   00102:     const d = new Date(start);
   00103:     d.setDate(start.getDate() + idx);
   00104:     const ymd = dateToYMDLocal(d);
   00105:     return {
   00106:       ymd,
   00107:       day: d.getDate(),
   00108:       inMonth: d.getMonth() === first.getMonth(),
   00109:     };
   00110:   });
   00111: }
   00112: 
   00113: function normalizeHourLike(value) {
   00114:   const s0 = safeStr(value);
   00115:   if (!s0) return "";
   00116: 
   00117:   const s = s0.replace(/\s+/g, "");
   00118: 
   00119:   const mhx = s.match(/^(\d{1,2})(?:h|hs|hr|hrs)$/i);
   00120:   if (mhx) return `${pad2(mhx[1])}:00`;
   00121: 
   00122:   const mISO = s.match(/^(\d{1,2}):(\d{1,2})(?::(\d{1,2}))?$/);
   00123:   if (mISO) return `${pad2(mISO[1])}:${pad2(mISO[2])}`;
   00124: 
   00125:   const m2 = s.match(/^(\d{1,2})$/);
   00126:   if (m2) return `${pad2(m2[1])}:00`;
   00127: 
   00128:   return s0;
   00129: }
   00130: 
   00131: function hourToNum(h) {
   00132:   const s = normalizeHourLike(h);
   00133:   const m = s.match(/^(\d{2}):(\d{2})$/);
   00134:   if (!m) return -1;
   00135:   return Number(m[1]) * 100 + Number(m[2]);

   00012:  * - 2 dígitos => dezena (últimos 2)  ✅ preserva zero à esquerda
   00013:  * - 3 dígitos => centena (últimos 3) ✅ preserva zero à esquerda
   00014:  * - 4 dígitos => milhar (4)         ✅ preserva zero à esquerda
   00015:  *
   00016:  * ✅ REGRA GLOBAL DO PROJETO:
   00017:  * - 7º prêmio = CENTENA (3 dígitos) e NÃO pode virar 4 dígitos.
   00018:  * - Nenhum zero à esquerda pode ser ignorado.
   00019:  *
   00020:  * ✅ UI:
   00021:  * - Mantém apenas 1 card, com 1 bloco: "OCORRÊNCIAS"
   00022:  *
   00023:  * ✅ PERF:
   00024:  * - Busca em chunks maiores (60 dias) + concorrência limitada
   00025:  * - Renderiza resultados de forma incremental
   00026:  */
   00027: 
   00028: function pad2(n) {
   00029:   return String(n).padStart(2, "0");
   00030: }
   00031: 
   00032: function safeStr(v) {
   00033:   return String(v ?? "").trim();
   00034: }
   00035: 
   00036: function isYMD(s) {
   00037:   return /^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/.test(String(s || "").trim());
   00038: }
   00039: 
   00040: /* =========================
   00041:    Bounds helpers (robusto)
   00042: ========================= */
   00043: 
   00044: function normalizeBoundsResponse(b) {
>> 00045:   const minRaw = safeStr(b?.minYmd || b?.minDate || b?.min || "");
>> 00046:   const maxRaw = safeStr(b?.maxYmd || b?.maxDate || b?.max || "");
   00047:   const minYmd = isYMD(minRaw) ? minRaw : null;
   00048:   const maxYmd = isYMD(maxRaw) ? maxRaw : null;
   00049:   return { minYmd, maxYmd, source: safeStr(b?.source || "") };
   00050: }
   00051: 
   00052: function clampYmd(ymd, minYmd, maxYmd) {
   00053:   const d = safeStr(ymd);
   00054:   if (!isYMD(d)) return "";
   00055:   let out = d;
   00056: 
   00057:   const min = safeStr(minYmd);
   00058:   const max = safeStr(maxYmd);
   00059: 
   00060:   if (isYMD(min) && out < min) out = min;
   00061:   if (isYMD(max) && out > max) out = max;
   00062: 
   00063:   return out;
   00064: }
   00065: 
   00066: function normalizeDigitsOnly(v) {
   00067:   return String(v ?? "").replace(/\D+/g, "");
   00068: }
   00069: 
   00070: /**
   00071:  * ✅ Extrai os ÚLTIMOS N dígitos e preserva zero à esquerda.
   00072:  * - Não converte para Number em nenhum momento.
   00073:  */
   00074: function extractLastNDigits(v, n) {
   00075:   const digits = normalizeDigitsOnly(v);
   00076:   if (!digits) return null;
   00077:   const last = digits.slice(-n).padStart(n, "0");
   00078:   const re = new RegExp(`^\\d{${n}}$`);
   00079:   return re.test(last) ? last : null;
   00080: }
   00081: 
   00082: function normalizeHourLike(value) {
   00083:   const s0 = String(value ?? "").trim();
   00084:   if (!s0) return "";
   00085:   const s = s0.replace(/\s+/g, "");
   00086: 
   00087:   const mhx = s.match(/^(\d{1,2})(?:h|hs|hr|hrs)$/i);
   00088:   if (mhx) return `${pad2(mhx[1])}:00`;
   00089: 
   00090:   const mISO = s.match(/^(\d{1,2}):(\d{1,2})(?::(\d{1,2}))?$/);
   00091:   if (mISO) return `${pad2(mISO[1])}:${pad2(mISO[2])}`;
   00092: 
   00093:   const m2 = s.match(/^(\d{1,2})$/);
   00094:   if (m2) return `${pad2(m2[1])}:00`;
   00095: 
   00096:   return s0.trim();
   00097: }
   00098: 
   00099: // Bucket "09h", "11h"…
   00100: function toHourBucket(value) {
   00101:   const s = String(value ?? "").trim();
   00102:   if (!s || s.toLowerCase() === "todos") return null;
   00103: 
   00104:   const mh = s.match(/^(\d{1,2})h$/i);
   00105:   if (mh) return `${pad2(mh[1])}h`;
   00106: 
   00107:   const m1 = s.match(/^(\d{1,2}):(\d{2})$/);
   00108:   if (m1) return `${pad2(m1[1])}h`;
   00109: 
   00110:   const m2 = s.match(/^(\d{1,2})$/);
   00111:   if (m2) return `${pad2(m2[1])}h`;
   00112: 
   00113:   const m3 = s.match(/^(\d{1,2}):(\d{2}):(\d{2})$/);
   00114:   if (m3) return `${pad2(m3[1])}h`;
   00115: 
   00116:   return null;
   00117: }
   00118: 
   00119: // Dia da semana por UTC
   00120: function dowFromYmdUTC(ymd) {
   00121:   if (!isYMD(ymd)) return "";
   00122:   const [y, m, d] = ymd.split("-").map((x) => Number(x));
   00123:   const dt = new Date(Date.UTC(y, m - 1, d));
   00124:   const dow = dt.getUTCDay();
   00125:   const labels = ["Dom", "Seg", "Ter", "Qua", "Qui", "Sex", "Sáb"];
   00126:   return labels[dow] || "";
   00127: }

   00130:   if (!isYMD(ymd)) return "";

   00490: /* =========================
   00491:    Pool (concorrência limitada)

   00493: 
   00494: async function runPool(items, worker, concurrency = 3) {
   00495:   const queue = [...items];
>> 00496:   const runners = Array.from({ length: Math.max(1, concurrency) }, async () => {
   00497:     while (queue.length) {
   00498:       const it = queue.shift();
   00499:       // eslint-disable-next-line no-await-in-loop
   00500:       await worker(it);
   00501:     }
   00502:   });
   00503:   await Promise.all(runners);
   00504: }

   00108: export function computeChainTransitions(draws) {
   00109:   const list = dedupeAndSortDraws(draws);
   00110:   const transitionsAny = new Map();
   00111:   const transitionsByEdge = new Map();
   00112: 
   00113:   for (let i = 0; i < list.length - 1; i += 1) {
   00114:     const fromDraw = list[i];
   00115:     const toDraw = list[i + 1];
   00116: 
   00117:     const fromGrupo = Number(pickPrize1GrupoFromDraw(fromDraw));
   00118:     const toGrupo = Number(pickPrize1GrupoFromDraw(toDraw));
   00119:     const fromHour = toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(fromDraw));
   00120:     const toHour = toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(toDraw));
   00121: 
   00122:     if (!Number.isFinite(fromGrupo) || !Number.isFinite(toGrupo)) continue;
   00123:     if (fromGrupo < 1 || fromGrupo > 25) continue;
   00124:     if (toGrupo < 1 || toGrupo > 25) continue;
   00125:     if (!fromHour || !toHour) continue;
   00126: 
   00127:     const anyRow = getNestedMap(transitionsAny, fromGrupo);
   00128:     anyRow.set(toGrupo, safeInt(anyRow.get(toGrupo), 0) + 1);
   00129: 
   00130:     const edgeKey = makeEdgeKey(fromHour, toHour);
   00131:     if (!edgeKey) continue;
   00132: 
   00133:     const edgeMap = getNestedMap(transitionsByEdge, edgeKey);
   00134:     const edgeRow = getNestedMap(edgeMap, fromGrupo);
   00135:     edgeRow.set(toGrupo, safeInt(edgeRow.get(toGrupo), 0) + 1);
   00136:   }
   00137: 
   00138:   return { transitionsAny, transitionsByEdge };
   00139: }

   02160: function buildRecentRepeatBoostMap(
   02161:   comparableSeq,
   02162:   targetTs,
   02163:   windowSize = 4,
   02164:   groupsK = TOP3_GROUPS_K
   02165: ) {
   02166:   const seq = getComparableRecentDrawsBeforeTs(
   02167:     Array.isArray(comparableSeq) ? comparableSeq : [],
   02168:     targetTs,
   02169:     windowSize
   02170:   );
   02171: 
   02172:   const k = safeInt(groupsK, 25);
   02173:   const counts = new Map();
   02174:   for (let g = 1; g <= k; g += 1) counts.set(g, 0);
   02175: 
   02176:   for (const item of seq) {
   02177:     const g = Number(item?.grupo);
   02178:     if (!Number.isFinite(g) || g < 1 || g > k) continue;
   02179:     counts.set(g, Number(counts.get(g) || 0) + 1);
   02180:   }
   02181: 
   02182:   const boost = new Map();
>> 02183:   const maxCount = Math.max(1, ...Array.from(counts.values()).map((n) => Number(n || 0)));
   02184: 
   02185:   for (let g = 1; g <= k; g += 1) {
   02186:     const c = Number(counts.get(g) || 0);
   02187:     boost.set(g, c >= 2 ? c / maxCount : 0);
   02188:   }
   02189: 
   02190:   return {
   02191:     boost,
   02192:     counts,
   02193:     windowSize,
   02194:     considered: Array.isArray(seq) ? seq.length : 0,
   02195:   };
   02196: }

   03327: function compareScenes(a, b, explain = false) {
   03328:   if (!a || !b) return explain ? { score: 0, details: {} } : 0;
   03329: 
   03330:   let score = 0;
   03331:   const details = {};
   03332: 
   03333:   const add = (key, value) => {
   03334:     if (!value) return;
   03335:     score += value;
   03336:     details[key] = value;
   03337:   };
   03338: 
   03339:   const ag = Array.isArray(a.grupos) ? a.grupos : [];
   03340:   const bg = Array.isArray(b.grupos) ? b.grupos : [];
   03341: 
   03342:   const ad = Array.isArray(a.dezenas) ? a.dezenas : [];
   03343:   const bd = Array.isArray(b.dezenas) ? b.dezenas : [];
   03344: 
   03345:   const ac = Array.isArray(a.centenas) ? a.centenas : [];
   03346:   const bc = Array.isArray(b.centenas) ? b.centenas : [];
   03347: 
   03348:   if (Number(a.firstGrupo) === Number(b.firstGrupo))
   03349:     add("firstGrupo",45);
   03350: 
   03351:   for (let i = 0; i < Math.min(ag.length,bg.length,7); i++) {
   03352:     if (Number(ag[i]) === Number(bg[i]))
   03353:       add("order_"+i, i===0 ? 20 : 10);
   03354:   }
   03355: 
   03356:   add(
   03357:     "groupOverlap",
   03358:     new Set(ag.filter(g => bg.includes(g))).size * 6
   03359:   );
   03360: 
   03361:   add(
   03362:     "dezenaOverlap",
   03363:     new Set(ad.filter(d => bd.includes(d))).size * 3
   03364:   );
   03365: 
   03366:   add(
   03367:     "centenaOverlap",
   03368:     new Set(ac.filter(c => bc.includes(c))).size * 1.5
   03369:   );
   03370: 
   03371:   const repA = Array.isArray(a.repeatedGroups) ? a.repeatedGroups.length : 0;
   03372:   const repB = Array.isArray(b.repeatedGroups) ? b.repeatedGroups.length : 0;
   03373: 
   03374:   if (repA > 0 && repB > 0)
   03375:     add("repeatExists",8);
   03376: 
   03377:   if (repA === repB && repA > 0)
   03378:     add("repeatEqual",6);
   03379: 
   03380:   if (String(a.signature||"") === String(b.signature||""))
   03381:     add("signature",60);
   03382: 
   03383:   if (a.hour === b.hour)
   03384:     add("hour",8);
   03385: 
   03386:   return explain ? { score, details } : score;
   03387: }

   03327: function compareScenes(a, b, explain = false) {
   03328:   if (!a || !b) return explain ? { score: 0, details: {} } : 0;
   03329: 
   03330:   let score = 0;
   03331:   const details = {};
   03332: 
   03333:   const add = (key, value) => {
   03334:     if (!value) return;
   03335:     score += value;
   03336:     details[key] = value;
   03337:   };
   03338: 
   03339:   const ag = Array.isArray(a.grupos) ? a.grupos : [];
   03340:   const bg = Array.isArray(b.grupos) ? b.grupos : [];
   03341: 
   03342:   const ad = Array.isArray(a.dezenas) ? a.dezenas : [];
   03343:   const bd = Array.isArray(b.dezenas) ? b.dezenas : [];
   03344: 
   03345:   const ac = Array.isArray(a.centenas) ? a.centenas : [];
   03346:   const bc = Array.isArray(b.centenas) ? b.centenas : [];
   03347: 
   03348:   if (Number(a.firstGrupo) === Number(b.firstGrupo))
   03349:     add("firstGrupo",45);
   03350: 
   03351:   for (let i = 0; i < Math.min(ag.length,bg.length,7); i++) {
   03352:     if (Number(ag[i]) === Number(bg[i]))
   03353:       add("order_"+i, i===0 ? 20 : 10);
   03354:   }
   03355: 
   03356:   add(
   03357:     "groupOverlap",
   03358:     new Set(ag.filter(g => bg.includes(g))).size * 6
   03359:   );
   03360: 
   03361:   add(
   03362:     "dezenaOverlap",
   03363:     new Set(ad.filter(d => bd.includes(d))).size * 3
   03364:   );
   03365: 
   03366:   add(
   03367:     "centenaOverlap",
   03368:     new Set(ac.filter(c => bc.includes(c))).size * 1.5
   03369:   );
   03370: 
   03371:   const repA = Array.isArray(a.repeatedGroups) ? a.repeatedGroups.length : 0;
   03372:   const repB = Array.isArray(b.repeatedGroups) ? b.repeatedGroups.length : 0;
   03373: 
   03374:   if (repA > 0 && repB > 0)
   03375:     add("repeatExists",8);
   03376: 
   03377:   if (repA === repB && repA > 0)
   03378:     add("repeatEqual",6);
   03379: 
   03380:   if (String(a.signature||"") === String(b.signature||""))
   03381:     add("signature",60);
   03382: 
   03383:   if (a.hour === b.hour)
   03384:     add("hour",8);
   03385: 
   03386:   return explain ? { score, details } : score;
   03387: }

   02304:   }

   03397: function compareScenes(a, b, explain = false) {
   03398:   if (!a || !b) return explain ? { score: 0, details: {} } : 0;
   03399: 
   03400:   let score = 0;
   03401:   const details = {};
   03402: 
   03403:   const add = (key, value) => {
   03404:     if (!value) return;
   03405:     score += value;
   03406:     details[key] = value;
   03407:   };
   03408: 
   03409:   const ag = Array.isArray(a.grupos) ? a.grupos : [];
   03410:   const bg = Array.isArray(b.grupos) ? b.grupos : [];
   03411: 
   03412:   const ad = Array.isArray(a.dezenas) ? a.dezenas : [];
   03413:   const bd = Array.isArray(b.dezenas) ? b.dezenas : [];
   03414: 
   03415:   const ac = Array.isArray(a.centenas) ? a.centenas : [];
   03416:   const bc = Array.isArray(b.centenas) ? b.centenas : [];
   03417: 
   03418:   if (Number(a.firstGrupo) === Number(b.firstGrupo))
   03419:     add("firstGrupo",45);
   03420: 
   03421:   for (let i = 0; i < Math.min(ag.length,bg.length,7); i++) {
   03422:     if (Number(ag[i]) === Number(bg[i]))
   03423:       add("order_"+i, i===0 ? 20 : 10);
   03424:   }
   03425: 
   03426:   add(
   03427:     "groupOverlap",
   03428:     new Set(ag.filter(g => bg.includes(g))).size * 6
   03429:   );
   03430: 
   03431:   add(
   03432:     "dezenaOverlap",
   03433:     new Set(ad.filter(d => bd.includes(d))).size * 3
   03434:   );
   03435: 
   03436:   add(
   03437:     "centenaOverlap",
   03438:     new Set(ac.filter(c => bc.includes(c))).size * 1.5
   03439:   );
   03440: 
   03441:   const repA = Array.isArray(a.repeatedGroups) ? a.repeatedGroups.length : 0;
   03442:   const repB = Array.isArray(b.repeatedGroups) ? b.repeatedGroups.length : 0;
   03443: 
   03444:   if (repA > 0 && repB > 0)
   03445:     add("repeatExists",8);
   03446: 
   03447:   if (repA === repB && repA > 0)
   03448:     add("repeatEqual",6);
   03449: 
   03450:   if (String(a.signature||"") === String(b.signature||""))
   03451:     add("signature",60);
   03452: 
   03453:   if (a.hour === b.hour)
   03454:     add("hour",8);
   03455: 
   03456:   return explain ? { score, details } : score;
   03457: }

   03955:     lotteryKey,

   03964:       ymd: slot.targetYmd,
   03965:       hour: slot.targetHour,
   03966:       baseYmd: slot.baseYmd,
   03967:       baseHour: slot.baseHour,

   03973:         Number(slot?.top3?.[0]?.grupo) === Number(slot.resultGrupo),
   03974:       top3Hit: (Array.isArray(slot.top3) ? slot.top3 : [])

   03977:       historyStats: slot.historyStats || null,

   03983: function buildTimelineForDate({
   03984:   ymd,
   03985:   drawsToday,
   03986:   drawsRange,
   03987:   lotteryKey,
   03988:   PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_NORMAL,
   03989:   PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_WED_SAT,
   03990:   FEDERAL_SCHEDULE,
   03991: }) {
   03992:   const targetYmd = safeStr(ymd);
   03993:   const day = Array.isArray(drawsToday) ? [...drawsToday] : [];
   03994:   const range = Array.isArray(drawsRange) ? [...drawsRange] : [];
   03995: 
   03996:   if (!isYMD(targetYmd) || !range.length) return [];
   03997: 
   03998:   const schedule = getScheduleForLottery({
   03999:     lotteryKey,
   04000:     ymd: targetYmd,
   04001:     PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_NORMAL,
   04002:     PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_WED_SAT,
   04003:     FEDERAL_SCHEDULE,
   04004:   });
   04005: 
   04006:   const normalizedSchedule = (Array.isArray(schedule) ? schedule : [])
   04007:     .map(toHourBucket)
   04008:     .filter(Boolean);
   04009: 
   04010:   if (!normalizedSchedule.length) return [];
   04011: 
   04012:   const daySorted = day
   04013:     .filter((d) => isYMD(pickDrawYMD(d)) && toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(d)))
   04014:     .sort((a, b) => {
   04015:       const ta = ymdHourToTs(pickDrawYMD(a), toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(a)));
   04016:       const tb = ymdHourToTs(pickDrawYMD(b), toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(b)));
   04017:       return ta - tb;
   04018:     });
   04019: 
   04020:   const rangeSorted = range
   04021:     .filter((d) => isYMD(pickDrawYMD(d)) && toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(d)))
   04022:     .sort((a, b) => {
   04023:       const ta = ymdHourToTs(pickDrawYMD(a), toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(a)));
   04024:       const tb = ymdHourToTs(pickDrawYMD(b), toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(b)));
   04025:       return ta - tb;
   04026:     });

   00101: function drawKey(draw) {

   00110: 
   00111:   return (Array.isArray(draws) ? draws : []).some((d) => {
   00112:     const h = toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(d));
   00113:     return h === target;
   00114:   });

   00120: 
   00121:   if (!key) return list;

   00123:   const map = new Map();
   00124: 
   00125:   for (const d of list) {
   00126:     const k = drawKey(d);
   00127:     if (k) map.set(k, d);
   00128:   }

   00130:   if (!map.has(key)) {
   00131:     map.set(key, baseDraw);
   00132:   }
   00133:

   00158: function findLatestHistoricalBaseDraw({
   00159:   draws,
   00160:   lotteryKey,
   00161:   targetYmd,
   00162:   targetHourBucket,
   00163: }) {
   00164:   const list = Array.isArray(draws) ? draws : [];
   00165:   const targetHour = toHourBucket(targetHourBucket);
   00166: 
   00167:   if (!isYMD(targetYmd) || !targetHour) {
   00168:     return { draw: null, ymd: "", hour: "", source: "none" };
   00169:   }
   00170: 
   00171:   const [Y, M, D] = String(targetYmd).split("-").map(Number);
   00172:   const mins = hourToInt(targetHour);
   00173:   const targetTs = Date.UTC(Y, M - 1, D) + mins * 60 * 1000;
   00174: 
   00175:   let best = null;
   00176:   let bestTs = Number.NEGATIVE_INFINITY;
   00177: 
   00178:   for (const d of list) {
   00179:     if (!isDrawValidForLotterySchedule(d, lotteryKey)) continue;
   00180: 
   00181:     const ts = drawTs(d);
   00182:     if (!Number.isFinite(ts)) continue;
   00183:     if (ts >= targetTs) continue;
   00184: 
   00185:     if (ts > bestTs) {
   00186:       best = d;
   00187:       bestTs = ts;
   00188:     }
   00189:   }
   00190: 
   00191:   if (!best) {
   00192:     return { draw: null, ymd: "", hour: "", source: "none" };
   00193:   }
   00194: 
   00195:   return {
   00196:     draw: best,
   00197:     ymd: pickDrawYMD(best) || "",
   00198:     hour: toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(best)) || "",
   00199:     source: "history_range",
   00200:   };
   00201: }
   00202: 
   00203: function sanitizeHistoricalDraws({ draws, lotteryKey, baseDraw }) {
   00204:   const list = Array.isArray(draws) ? draws : [];
   00205:   const baseTs = drawTs(baseDraw);
   00206: 
   00207:   if (!baseDraw || !Number.isFinite(baseTs)) return [];
   00208: 
   00209:   return list
   00210:     .filter((d) => isDrawValidForLotterySchedule(d, lotteryKey))
   00211:     .filter((d) => {
   00212:       const ts = drawTs(d);
   00213:       return Number.isFinite(ts) && ts <= baseTs;
   00214:     })
   00215:     .sort((a, b) => drawTs(a) - drawTs(b));
   00216: }

   00028: export function buildResultStyleImgVariants(grupo, size = 96) {
   00029:   const g = Number(grupo);
   00030:   if (!Number.isFinite(g) || g < 1 || g > 25) return [];
   00031: 
   00032:   const seeds = [getImgFromGrupo?.(g, size), getImgFromGrupo?.(g)]
   00033:     .map(normalizeImgSrc)
   00034:     .filter(Boolean);
   00035: 
   00036:   const out = [];
   00037: 
   00038:   for (const seed of seeds) {
   00039:     const clean = String(seed).split("?")[0];
   00040:     if (!clean) continue;
   00041: 
   00042:     out.push(clean);
   00043: 
   00044:     if (/\.png$/i.test(clean)) {
   00045:       out.push(clean.replace(/\.png$/i, ".jpg"));
   00046:       out.push(clean.replace(/\.png$/i, ".jpeg"));
   00047:       out.push(clean.replace(/\.png$/i, ".webp"));
   00048:     } else if (/\.jpg$/i.test(clean)) {
   00049:       out.push(clean.replace(/\.jpg$/i, ".png"));
   00050:       out.push(clean.replace(/\.jpg$/i, ".jpeg"));
   00051:       out.push(clean.replace(/\.jpg$/i, ".webp"));
   00052:     } else if (/\.jpeg$/i.test(clean)) {
   00053:       out.push(clean.replace(/\.jpeg$/i, ".png"));
   00054:       out.push(clean.replace(/\.jpeg$/i, ".jpg"));
   00055:       out.push(clean.replace(/\.jpeg$/i, ".webp"));
   00056:     } else if (/\.webp$/i.test(clean)) {
   00057:       out.push(clean.replace(/\.webp$/i, ".png"));
   00058:       out.push(clean.replace(/\.webp$/i, ".jpg"));
   00059:       out.push(clean.replace(/\.webp$/i, ".jpeg"));
   00060:     }
   00061:   }
   00062: 
>> 00063:   return Array.from(new Set(out.filter(Boolean)));
   00064: }

   00024: function makeKey(ymd, hour) {
   00025:   const y = safeStr(ymd);
   00026:   const h = normHour(hour);
   00027:   return isYMD(y) && h ? `${y}_${h}` : "";
   00028: }
   00029: 
   00030: function parseTargetDate(ymd, hour) {
   00031:   if (!isYMD(ymd)) return null;
   00032: 
   00033:   const hourBucket = normHour(hour);
   00034:   const m = String(hourBucket || "").match(/^(\d{2})h$/);
   00035:   if (!m) return null;
   00036: 
   00037:   const [Y, M, D] = String(ymd).split("-").map(Number);
   00038:   const hh = Number(m[1]);
   00039: 
   00040:   const dt = new Date(Y, M - 1, D, hh, 0, 0, 0);
   00041:   return Number.isNaN(dt.getTime()) ? null : dt;
   00042: }
   00043: 
   00044: function isFutureTarget(ymd, hour) {
   00045:   const target = parseTargetDate(ymd, hour);
   00046:   if (!target) return false;
   00047:   return target.getTime() > Date.now();
   00048: }
   00049: 
   00050: function resolvePendingStatus(ymd, hour, picks) {
   00051:   const hasPicks = Array.isArray(picks) && picks.length > 0;
   00052:   if (!hasPicks) return "empty";
   00053:   return isFutureTarget(ymd, hour) ? "future" : "pending_result";
   00054: }
   00055: 
   00056: function normalizePicks(picks) {
>> 00057:   return Array.from(

   00062:     )
   00063:   ).slice(0, 3);

   00066: function normalizeResult(resultValue) {

   00069: }
   00070: 
   00071: function normalizeLogEntry(entry) {
   00072:   if (!entry || typeof entry !== "object") return null;
   00073: 
   00074:   const ymd = safeStr(entry?.target?.ymd || "");
   00075:   const hour = normHour(entry?.target?.hour || "");
   00076:   const targetKey = makeKey(ymd, hour);
   00077: 
   00078:   if (!targetKey) return null;
   00079: 
   00080:   const picks = normalizePicks(entry?.picks);
   00081:   const result = normalizeResult(entry?.result);
   00082: 
   00083:   const hit =
   00084:     result != null && picks.length
   00085:       ? picks.includes(result)
   00086:       : typeof entry?.hit === "boolean"
   00087:         ? entry.hit
   00088:         : null;
   00089: 
   00090:   const status =
   00091:     result != null ? "validated" : resolvePendingStatus(ymd, hour, picks);
   00092: 
   00093:   return {
   00094:     ...entry,
   00095:     targetKey,
   00096:     target: { ymd, hour },
   00097:     picks,
   00098:     result,
   00099:     hit,
   00100:     createdAt: Number(entry?.createdAt) || Date.now(),
   00101:     validatedAt:
   00102:       result != null && Number(entry?.validatedAt)
   00103:         ? Number(entry.validatedAt)
   00104:         : undefined,
   00105:     status,
   00106:   };
   00107: }
   00108: 
   00109: function scoreLogEntry(entry) {
   00110:   if (!entry) return -1;
   00111: 
   00112:   let score = 0;
   00113:   if (Array.isArray(entry?.picks) && entry.picks.length) score += 20;
   00114:   if (entry?.result != null) score += 10;
   00115:   if (typeof entry?.hit === "boolean") score += 5;
   00116:   if (entry?.validatedAt) score += 2;
   00117:   if (entry?.createdAt) score += 1;
   00118: 
   00119:   return score;
   00120: }
   00121: 
   00122: function normalizeAndDedupeLog(log) {
   00123:   const list = Array.isArray(log) ? log : [];
   00124:   const byKey = new Map();
   00125: 
   00126:   for (const raw of list) {
   00127:     const entry = normalizeLogEntry(raw);
   00128:     if (!entry) continue;
   00129: 
   00130:     const prev = byKey.get(entry.targetKey);
   00131: 
   00132:     if (!prev || scoreLogEntry(entry) >= scoreLogEntry(prev)) {
   00133:       byKey.set(entry.targetKey, entry);
   00134:     }
   00135:   }
   00136: 
   00137:   return [...byKey.values()].sort((a, b) => {
   00138:     const aKey = `${a?.target?.ymd || ""}_${a?.target?.hour || ""}`;
   00139:     const bKey = `${b?.target?.ymd || ""}_${b?.target?.hour || ""}`;
   00140:     return aKey < bKey ? -1 : aKey > bKey ? 1 : 0;
   00141:   });
   00142: }
   00143: 
   00144: function getTop3Log() {
   00145:   try {
   00146:     const raw = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem("top3_log") || "[]");
   00147:     return normalizeAndDedupeLog(raw);
   00148:   } catch {
   00149:     return [];
   00150:   }
   00151: }
   00152: 
   00153: function saveTop3Log(log) {
   00154:   try {

   00156:     localStorage.setItem("top3_log", JSON.stringify(normalized));
   00157:   } catch {}
   00158: }

   00109: function top3ItemMatchesSlot(item, slot) {
   00110:   const targetY = safeStr(slot?.targetYmd || "");
   00111:   const targetH = toHourBucket(slot?.targetHour || "");
   00112: 
   00113:   const nextY = safeStr(item?.meta?.next?.ymd || "");
   00114:   const nextH = toHourBucket(item?.meta?.next?.hour || "");
   00115: 
   00116:   if (!isYMD(targetY) || !targetH) return false;
   00117:   if (!isYMD(nextY) || !nextH) return false;
   00118: 
   00119:   return nextY === targetY && nextH === targetH;
   00120: }

   00072: function buildFrequencyEvidence(item = {}, context = {}) {
   00073:   const frequency = normalize(
   00074:     item.frequency ??
   00075:     item.total ??
   00076:     item.count ??
   00077:     item.appearances

   00083:     context.sampleSize
   00084:   );

   00087:     totalUniverse > 0
   00088:       ? normalizePercent((frequency / totalUniverse) * 100)
   00089:       : normalizePercent(
   00090:           item.frequencyPercent ??
   00091:           item.frequencyRate
   00092:         );
   00093: 
   00094:   const level = classifyFrequency(percent);
   00095: 
   00096:   return {
   00097:     module: "frequency",

   00111:       hour: context.hour || null,
   00112:     },

   00388: export function auditDatasetSummary(drawsRaw, options = {}) {
   00389:   const draws = dedupeDraws(drawsRaw);
   00390:   const sampleLimit = Number(options?.sampleLimit ?? 20);
   00391: 
   00392:   let drawsWithPrizes = 0;
   00393:   let drawsWithoutPrizes = 0;
   00394: 
   00395:   let prizesTotal = 0;
   00396: 
   00397:   let invalidDateCount = 0;
   00398:   let invalidCloseHourCount = 0;
   00399: 
   00400:   let invalidGrupoCount = 0;
   00401:   let missingGrupoCount = 0;
   00402: 
   00403:   let missingPositionCount = 0;
   00404:   let invalidPositionCount = 0;
   00405: 
   00406:   for (const d of draws) {
   00407:     const prizes = Array.isArray(d?.prizes) ? d.prizes : [];
   00408: 
   00409:     if (prizes.length) drawsWithPrizes += 1;
   00410:     else drawsWithoutPrizes += 1;
   00411: 
   00412:     const ymd = normalizeToYMD(extractRawDateFromDraw(d));
   00413:     if (!ymd) invalidDateCount += 1;
   00414: 
   00415:     const hhmm = normalizeHourLike(extractRawCloseHourFromDraw(d));
   00416:     if (!hhmm || !isValidHHMM(hhmm)) invalidCloseHourCount += 1;
   00417: 
   00418:     prizesTotal += prizes.length;
   00419: 
   00420:     for (const p of prizes) {
   00421:       const gNum = Number(p?.grupo ?? p?.group);
   00422:       if (!Number.isFinite(gNum)) {
   00423:         missingGrupoCount += 1;
   00424:       } else if (gNum < 1 || gNum > 25) {
   00425:         invalidGrupoCount += 1;
   00426:       }
   00427: 
   00428:       const posRaw = p?.posicao ?? p?.position;
   00429:       if (posRaw === null || posRaw === undefined || posRaw === "") {
   00430:         missingPositionCount += 1;
   00431:       } else {
   00432:         const pos = Number(posRaw);
   00433:         if (!Number.isFinite(pos) || pos < 1 || pos > 10) {
   00434:           invalidPositionCount += 1;
   00435:         }
   00436:       }
   00437:     }
   00438:   }
   00439: 
   00440:   const byGrupo = auditCountsByGrupo(draws, {
   00441:     position: options?.position ?? null,
   00442:     hour: options?.hour ?? null,
   00443:     matchHourOnly: options?.matchHourOnly ?? false,
   00444:   });
   00445: 
   00446:   const animalMismatchCount = byGrupo.integrityIssues.length;
   00447:   const integrityIssuesSample = byGrupo.integrityIssues.slice(
   00448:     0,
   00449:     Math.max(0, Number.isFinite(sampleLimit) ? sampleLimit : 20)
   00450:   );
   00451: 
   00452:   const totals = computeDrawTotals(draws);
   00453: 
   00454:   return {
   00455:     totals: {
   00456:       draws: draws.length,
>> 00457:       uniqueDays: totals.uniqueDays,
   00458:       drawsWithPrizes,
   00459:       drawsWithoutPrizes,
   00460:       prizesTotal,
   00461:     },
   00462:     issues: {
   00463:       invalidDateCount,
   00464:       invalidCloseHourCount,
   00465:       missingGrupoCount,
   00466:       invalidGrupoCount,
   00467:       missingPositionCount,
   00468:       invalidPositionCount,
   00469:       animalMismatchCount,
   00470:     },
   00471:     byGrupo: {
   00472:       rowsByGrupo: byGrupo.rowsByGrupo,
   00473:       rowsSorted: byGrupo.rows,
   00474:       totalOcorrencias: byGrupo.totalOcorrencias,
   00475:       appliedFilters: byGrupo.appliedFilters,
   00476:     },
   00477:     integrityIssuesSample,
   00478:     auditOptions: {

   00480:       hour: options?.hour ?? null,

   00442: async function mapWithConcurrency(items, limitN, mapper) {
   00443:   const arr = Array.isArray(items) ? items : [];
   00444:   const concurrency = Math.max(1, Number(limitN) || 6);
   00445:   const results = new Array(arr.length);
   00446:   let idx = 0;
   00447: 
   00448:   async function worker() {
   00449:     while (true) {
   00450:       const current = idx;
   00451:       idx += 1;
   00452:       if (current >= arr.length) break;
   00453:       results[current] = await mapper(arr[current], current);
   00454:     }
   00455:   }
   00456: 
   00457:   await Promise.all(

   00467: function isIndexError(error) {
   00468:   const msg = String(error?.message || "").toLowerCase();
   00469:   const code = String(error?.code || "").toLowerCase();
   00470:   return (
   00471:     code.includes("failed-precondition") ||
   00472:     msg.includes("failed_precondition") ||
   00473:     (msg.includes("index") && msg.includes("create")) ||
   00474:     (msg.includes("requires") && msg.includes("index"))
   00475:   );
   00476: }

   00491: /**
   00492:  * ✅ FIX DEFINITIVO:

   00494:  * faz fallback e busca a subcollection draws/{id}/prizes.

   00496: async function fetchPrizesForDraw(drawId, positionsArr, embeddedPrizes) {
   00497:   const drawKey = String(drawId || "").trim();
   00498:   if (!drawKey) return [];
   00499: 
   00500:   if (Array.isArray(embeddedPrizes) && embeddedPrizes.length) {
   00501:     const normalized = embeddedPrizes.map((p, idx) =>
   00502:       normalizePrize(p, p?.prizeId ?? `emb_${idx}`)
   00503:     );
   00504: 
   00505:     const cleaned = normalized.filter(
   00506:       (x) => isValidGrupo(x?.grupo) && isValidPosition(x?.position)
   00507:     );
   00508: 
   00509:     if (cleaned.length) {
   00510:       const allSorted = sortPrizesByPosition(cleaned);
   00511:       return filterPrizesByPositions(allSorted, positionsArr);
   00512:     }
   00513:   }
   00514: 
   00515:   const allKey = prizesCacheKeyAll(drawKey);
   00516:   const cachedAll = cacheGet(PRIZES_CACHE, allKey);
   00517:   if (Array.isArray(cachedAll) && cachedAll.length > 0) {
   00518:     return filterPrizesByPositions(cachedAll, positionsArr);
   00519:   }
   00520: 
   00521:   const prizesCol = collection(db, "draws", drawKey, "prizes");
   00522: 
   00523:   const { snap, error } = await safeGetDocsSmart(prizesCol, { policy: "server" });
   00524:   if (error) throw error;
   00525: 
   00526:   const allRaw = snap.docs.map((d) => normalizePrize(d.data(), d.id));
   00527: 
   00528:   // console.log("DEBUG allRaw:", allRaw);
   00529: 
   00530: const all = allRaw.filter(
   00531:   (x) => isValidGrupo(x?.grupo) && isValidPosition(x?.position)
   00532: );
   00533: 
   00534: // console.log("DEBUG allFiltered:", all);
   00535: 
   00536:   const allSorted = sortPrizesByPosition(all);
   00537:   cacheSet(PRIZES_CACHE, allKey, allSorted);
   00538: 
   00539:   return filterPrizesByPositions(allSorted, positionsArr);
   00540: }

   01000: /* =========================

   01003: 
   01004: function buildPrizeSignature(draw) {
   01005:   const prizes = Array.isArray(draw?.prizes) ? draw.prizes : [];
   01006:   if (!prizes.length) return "";
   01007: 
   01008:   const parts = prizes
   01009:     .map((p) => {
   01010:       const pos = Number(p?.position);
   01011:       const grupo = Number(p?.grupo);
   01012:       const numero = toPrizeDigitsByPosition(
   01013:         p?.numero ?? p?.milhar ?? p?.number ?? p?.num ?? p?.valor ?? "",
   01014:         pos
   01015:       );
   01016: 
   01017:       if (!Number.isFinite(pos) || !Number.isFinite(grupo) || !numero) return "";
   01018:       return `${pad2(pos)}:${pad2(grupo)}:${numero}`;
   01019:     })
   01020:     .filter(Boolean)
   01021:     .sort();
   01022: 
   01023:   return parts.join("|");
   01024: }
   01025: 
   01026: function federalHourPriority(draw) {
   01027:   const h = normalizeHourLike(
   01028:     draw?.close_hour || draw?.closeHour || draw?.hour || draw?.hora || ""
   01029:   );
   01030:   const ymd = String(draw?.ymd || normalizeToYMD(draw?.date) || "").trim();
   01031:   const n = hourToNumSafe(h);
   01032: 
   01033:   const prefer20h = getFederalPreferredHourForYmd(ymd) === "20:00";
   01034: 
   01035:   if (prefer20h) {
   01036:     if (h === "20:00") return 200000 + n;
   01037:     if (h === "19:00") return 190000 + n;
   01038:     return 100000 + n;
   01039:   }
   01040: 
   01041:   if (h === "19:00") return 200000 + n;
   01042:   if (h === "20:00") return 190000 + n;
   01043:   return 100000 + n;
   01044: }
   01045: 
   01046: function pickBetterFederalClone(a, b) {
   01047:   const pa = Number.isFinite(Number(a?.prizesCount))
   01048:     ? Number(a.prizesCount)
   01049:     : Array.isArray(a?.prizes)
   01050:     ? a.prizes.length
   01051:     : 0;
   01052: 
   01053:   const pb = Number.isFinite(Number(b?.prizesCount))
   01054:     ? Number(b.prizesCount)
   01055:     : Array.isArray(b?.prizes)
   01056:     ? b.prizes.length
   01057:     : 0;
   01058: 
   01059:   if (pa !== pb) return pb > pa ? b : a;
   01060: 
   01061:   const ha = federalHourPriority(a);
   01062:   const hb = federalHourPriority(b);
   01063:   if (ha !== hb) return hb > ha ? b : a;
   01064: 
   01065:   const ia = String(a?.drawId || a?.id || "");
   01066:   const ib = String(b?.drawId || b?.id || "");
   01067:   return ib.localeCompare(ia) > 0 ? b : a;
   01068: }
   01069: 
   01070: function collapseFederalDailyCloneDraws(draws) {
   01071:   const arr = Array.isArray(draws) ? draws : [];
   01072:   const byKey = new Map();
   01073: 
   01074:   for (const d of arr) {
   01075:     const ymd = String(d?.ymd || normalizeToYMD(d?.date) || "").trim();
   01076:     const sig = buildPrizeSignature(d);
   01077: 
   01078:     if (!ymd || !sig) {
   01079:       const fallbackKey = `id::${String(
   01080:         d?.drawId || d?.id || Math.random()
   01081:       )}`;
   01082:       if (!byKey.has(fallbackKey)) byKey.set(fallbackKey, d);
   01083:       continue;
   01084:     }
   01085: 
   01086:     const key = `${ymd}__${sig}`;
   01087:     const prev = byKey.get(key);
   01088: 
   01089:     if (!prev) byKey.set(key, d);
   01090:     else byKey.set(key, pickBetterFederalClone(prev, d));
   01091:   }
   01092: 
>> 01093:   return sortDrawsLocal(Array.from(byKey.values()));
   01094: }

   01096: function drawsCacheKeyDay({ scopeKey, ymd, positionsArr, hourFilter }) {
   01097:   const p = positionsArr && positionsArr.length ? positionsArr.join(",") : "all";

   01102:       ? `exact:${hourFilter.hhmm}`
   01103:       : "all";
   01104:   return `day::${scopeKey}::${ymd}::pos=${p}::h=${h}`;

   01534: export async function hydrateKingDrawsWithPrizes({
   01535:   draws,
   01536:   positions = null,
   01537:   concurrency = null,
   01538: }) {

   01541: 
   01542:   const positionsArr = normalizePositions(positions);
   01543: 
   01544:   const conc =
   01545:     Number.isFinite(Number(concurrency)) && Number(concurrency) > 0
   01546:       ? Number(concurrency)
   01547:       : chooseRangeConcurrency(arr.length);
   01548: 
   01549:   const results = await mapWithConcurrency(arr, conc, async (item) => {
   01550:     const prizes = await fetchPrizesForDraw(item.drawId, positionsArr, item.prizes);
   01551:     const pc = Array.isArray(prizes) ? prizes.length : 0;
   01552:     return { ...item, prizes, prizesCount: pc, __mode: "detailed" };
   01553:   });
   01554: 
   01555:   return dedupeDrawsLocal(results);
   01556: }

   01562: function normalizeLotteriesList(lotteries) {

   01564:   const cleaned = arr
   01565:     .map((x) => String(x ?? "").trim().toUpperCase())

   01569: 
   01570: function drawPassesLotteriesFilter(draw, lotteriesArr) {
   01571:   if (!lotteriesArr || !lotteriesArr.length) return true;
   01572:   const code = String(draw?.lottery_code ?? "").trim().toUpperCase();
   01573:   if (!code) return false;
   01574:   return lotteriesArr.includes(code);

   01577: function lateCacheKey({
   01578:   scopeKey,
   01579:   fromYmd,
   01580:   toYmd,
   01581:   baseYmd,
   01582:   prizePosition,
   01583:   hourFilter,
   01584:   lotteriesArr,
   01585:   chunkDays,
   01586: }) {
   01587:   const h =
   01588:     hourFilter?.kind === "bucket"
   01589:       ? `bucket:${hourFilter.bucket}`
   01590:       : hourFilter?.kind === "exact"
   01591:       ? `exact:${hourFilter.hhmm}`
   01592:       : "all";
   01593:   const lots = lotteriesArr && lotteriesArr.length ? lotteriesArr.join(",") : "all";
   01594:   const cd = Number(chunkDays) || 15;
   01595:   const pos = Number(prizePosition) || 1;
   01596:   return `late::${scopeKey}::${fromYmd}..${toYmd}::base=${baseYmd}::pos=${pos}::h=${h}::lots=${lots}::chunk=${cd}`;
   01597: }
   01598: 
   01599: function hourToNumSafe(hhmm) {
   01600:   const s = normalizeHourLike(hhmm);
   01601:   const m = s.match(/^(\d{2}):(\d{2})$/);
   01602:   if (!m) return Number.POSITIVE_INFINITY;
   01603:   return Number(m[1]) * 100 + Number(m[2]);
   01604: }

   00050: export function validateRankingLineByLine(expectedCounts, rankingRows) {
   00051:   const issues = [];
   00052:   const gotMap = new Map();
   00053: 
   00054:   const ignoredExpectedKeys = [];
   00055:   const ignoredRows = [];
   00056: 
   00057:   // expectedCounts normalizado
   00058:   const expectedNorm = {};
   00059:   for (const [k, v] of Object.entries(expectedCounts || {})) {
   00060:     const g = normalizeGrupo(k);
   00061:     if (g === "00") {
   00062:       ignoredExpectedKeys.push(String(k));
   00063:       continue;
   00064:     }
   00065:     expectedNorm[g] = toNumber(v);
   00066:   }
   00067: 
   00068:   // soma rows por grupo
   00069:   for (const r of rankingRows || []) {
   00070:     const rawG = r?.grupo ?? r?.group;
   00071:     const g = normalizeGrupo(rawG);
   00072:     if (g === "00") {
   00073:       ignoredRows.push({ rawGrupo: rawG, row: r });
   00074:       continue;
   00075:     }
   00076: 
   00077:     const val = toNumber(r?.apar ?? r?.total ?? r?.count ?? r?.value ?? 0);
   00078:     gotMap.set(g, toNumber(gotMap.get(g)) + val);
   00079:   }
   00080: 
>> 00081:   const allGroups = Array.from({ length: 25 }, (_, i) => pad2(i + 1));
   00082: 
   00083:   let okCount = 0;
   00084:   let diffCount = 0;
   00085: 
   00086:   for (const g of allGroups) {
   00087:     const expected = toNumber(expectedNorm?.[g] ?? 0);
   00088:     const got = toNumber(gotMap.get(g) ?? 0);
   00089:     const delta = got - expected;
   00090: 
   00091:     const status = delta === 0 ? "OK" : "DIF";
   00092:     if (status === "OK") okCount += 1;
   00093:     else diffCount += 1;
   00094: 
   00095:     issues.push({ grupo: g, esperado: expected, obtido: got, delta, status });
   00096:   }
   00097: 
   00098:   const totalEsperado = allGroups.reduce(
   00099:     (acc, g) => acc + toNumber(expectedNorm?.[g] ?? 0),
   00100:     0
   00101:   );
   00102: 
>> 00103:   const totalObtido = Array.from(gotMap.values()).reduce(
   00104:     (acc, v) => acc + toNumber(v),
   00105:     0
   00106:   );
   00107: 
   00108:   const gotMapObj = {};
   00109:   for (const g of allGroups) gotMapObj[g] = toNumber(gotMap.get(g) ?? 0);
   00110: 
   00111:   return {
   00112:     issues,
   00113:     okCount,
   00114:     diffCount,
   00115:     totalEsperado,
   00116:     totalObtido,
   00117:     totalDelta: totalObtido - totalEsperado,
   00118:     gotMapObj,
   00119: 
   00120:     // extras p/ debug
   00121:     ignoredExpectedKeys,
   00122:     ignoredRowsCount: ignoredRows.length,
   00123:   };
   00124: }

   00007: export function normalizePositions(positions) {
   00008:   const arr =
   00009:     Array.isArray(positions) && positions.length
   00010:       ? positions
   00011:           .map(Number)
   00012:           .filter((n) => Number.isFinite(n) && n > 0)
   00013:       : null;
   00014: 
   00015:   if (!arr || !arr.length) return null;
>> 00016:   return Array.from(new Set(arr)).sort((a, b) => a - b);
   00017: }
   00018: 
   00019: export function isValidGrupo(n) {
   00020:   return Number.isFinite(Number(n)) && Number(n) >= 1 && Number(n) <= 25;
   00021: }
   00022: 
   00023: export function isValidPosition(n) {
   00024:   return Number.isFinite(Number(n)) && Number(n) >= 1 && Number(n) <= 10;
   00025: }
   00026: 
   00027: export function extractIntInRange(value, min, max) {
   00028:   if (value === null || value === undefined) return null;
   00029: 
   00030:   if (typeof value === "number" && Number.isFinite(value)) {
   00031:     const n = Math.trunc(value);
   00032:     return n >= min && n <= max ? n : null;
   00033:   }
   00034: 
   00035:   const s = String(value).trim();
   00036:   if (!s) return null;
   00037: 
   00038:   if (/^\d+$/.test(s)) {
   00039:     const n = Number(s);
   00040:     return Number.isFinite(n) && n >= min && n <= max ? n : null;
   00041:   }
   00042: 
   00043:   const m = s.match(/(\d{1,2})/);
   00044:   if (!m) return null;
   00045:   const n = Number(m[1]);
   00046:   return Number.isFinite(n) && n >= min && n <= max ? n : null;
   00047: }
   00048: 
   00049: export function normalizeDigitsOnly(v) {
   00050:   const s = String(v ?? "").trim();
   00051:   if (!s) return "";
   00052:   return s.replace(/\D+/g, "");
   00053: }
   00054: 
   00055: export function prizeWidthByPosition(position) {
   00056:   return Number(position) === 7 ? 3 : 4;
   00057: }
   00058: 
   00059: export function toPrizeDigitsByPosition(input, position) {
   00060:   const digits = normalizeDigitsOnly(input);
   00061:   if (!digits) return null;
   00062: 
   00063:   const w = prizeWidthByPosition(position);
   00064: 
   00065:   if (w === 3) {
   00066:     const last3 = digits.slice(-3).padStart(3, "0");
   00067:     return /^\d{3}$/.test(last3) ? last3 : null;
   00068:   }
   00069: 
   00070:   const last4 = digits.slice(-4).padStart(4, "0");
   00071:   return /^\d{4}$/.test(last4) ? last4 : null;
   00072: }
   00073: 
   00074: export function digitsToDezena2(numStr) {
   00075:   const s = String(numStr || "");
   00076:   if (!/^\d{2,4}$/.test(s)) return null;
   00077:   return s.slice(-2);
   00078: }
   00079: 
   00080: export function digitsToCentena3(numStr) {
   00081:   const s = String(numStr || "");
   00082:   if (!/^\d{3,4}$/.test(s)) return null;
   00083:   return s.slice(-3);
   00084: }

   00201:   }
   00202: 
   00203:   return out;
   00204: }
   00205: 
   00206: /* =========================
   00207:    Main
   00208: ========================= */
   00209: export function buildRanking(draws, opts = {}) {
   00210:   // ✅ correção: dedup no nível de draws ANTES de qualquer métrica
   00211:   const list = dedupeDraws(Array.isArray(draws) ? draws : []);
   00212: 
   00213:   // Padrão atual (mantido): conta por prize
   00214:   const countMode = String(opts.countMode || "byPrize"); // "byPrize" | "byDraw"
   00215:   const maxIntegrityIssues = Number.isFinite(Number(opts.maxIntegrityIssues))
   00216:     ? Math.max(0, Number(opts.maxIntegrityIssues))
   00217:     : 300;
   00218: 
>> 00219:   const totalDraws = list.length;
   00220: 
   00221:   // metas de período
   00222:   const daySet = new Set();
>> 00223:   let minDate = null;
>> 00224:   let maxDate = null;
   00225: 
   00226:   for (const d of list) {
   00227:     const ymd = normalizeToYMD(getDrawYmdRaw(d));
   00228:     if (!ymd) continue;
   00229: 
   00230:     daySet.add(ymd);
>> 00231:     if (!minDate || ymd < minDate) minDate = ymd;
>> 00232:     if (!maxDate || ymd > maxDate) maxDate = ymd;
   00233:   }
   00234: 
>> 00235:   const uniqueDays = daySet.size;
   00236: 
   00237:   // ranking
   00238:   const map = new Map();
   00239:   let totalOcorrencias = 0;
   00240: 
   00241:   // auditoria de consistência grupo x animal
   00242:   const integrityIssues = [];
   00243: 
   00244:   for (const draw of list) {
   00245:     const prizes = dedupePrizes(draw?.prizes);
   00246: 
   00247:     // se countMode = byDraw, evitamos contar o mesmo grupo mais de 1x por draw
   00248:     const countedGroupsThisDraw = countMode === "byDraw" ? new Set() : null;
   00249: 
   00250:     for (const prize of prizes) {
   00251:       const grupoRaw = prize?.grupo ?? prize?.group;
   00252:       const animalRaw = prize?.animal;
   00253: 
   00254:       if (grupoRaw == null) continue;
   00255: 
   00256:       const grupoNum = Number(grupoRaw);
   00257:       if (!Number.isFinite(grupoNum)) continue;
   00258:       if (grupoNum < 1 || grupoNum > 25) continue;
   00259: 
   00260:       if (countedGroupsThisDraw) {
   00261:         const k = String(grupoNum);
   00262:         if (countedGroupsThisDraw.has(k)) continue;
   00263:         countedGroupsThisDraw.add(k);
   00264:       }
   00265: 
   00266:       const official = getOfficialByGrupo(grupoNum);
   00267:       const grupo = official?.grupo || pad2(grupoNum);
   00268: 
   00269:       const animalFromData = animalRaw ? normalizeAnimalLocal(animalRaw) : "";
   00270:       const animalOfficial = official?.animal || "";
   00271:       const animalLabel = animalFromData || animalOfficial || "";
   00272: 
   00273:       let entry = map.get(grupo);
   00274:       if (!entry) {
   00275:         entry = {
   00276:           grupo,                 // "01".."25" (string)
   00277:           grupoNum: grupoNum,    // 1..25 (number)
   00278:           animal: animalLabel,   // "GATO"
   00279:           slug: official?.slug || "",
   00280:           dezenas: official?.dezenas || [],
   00281:           total: 0,
   00282:         };
   00283:         map.set(grupo, entry);
   00284:       } else {
   00285:         // completa metadados se necessário
   00286:         if (!entry.grupoNum) entry.grupoNum = grupoNum;
   00287:         if (!entry.slug && official?.slug) entry.slug = official.slug;
   00288:         if ((!entry.dezenas || !entry.dezenas.length) && official?.dezenas?.length) {
   00289:           entry.dezenas = official.dezenas;
   00290:         }
   00291:         if (!entry.animal && animalLabel) entry.animal = animalLabel;
   00292:       }
   00293: 
   00294:       // auditoria: se veio animal no dado e diverge do oficial, registra e “trava” no oficial
   00295:       if (
   00296:         animalFromData &&
   00297:         animalOfficial &&
   00298:         animalFromData !== animalOfficial &&
   00299:         integrityIssues.length < maxIntegrityIssues
   00300:       ) {
   00301:         integrityIssues.push({
   00302:           drawId: draw?.drawId ?? draw?.id ?? null,
   00303:           date: getDrawYmdRaw(draw),
   00304:           close_hour: getDrawHourRaw(draw) || null,
   00305:           grupo,
   00306:           grupoNum,
   00307:           animal_no_dado: animalRaw,
   00308:           animal_normalizado: animalFromData,
   00309:           animal_oficial: animalOfficial,
   00310:         });
   00311: 
   00312:         if (animalOfficial) entry.animal = animalOfficial;
   00313:       }
   00314: 
   00315:       entry.total += 1;
   00316:       totalOcorrencias += 1;
   00317:     }
   00318:   }
   00319: 
   00320:   // garante 01..25 sempre presentes
   00321:   for (let i = 1; i <= 25; i += 1) {
   00322:     const official = getOfficialByGrupo(i);
   00323:     const g = official?.grupo || pad2(i);
   00324: 
   00325:     let entry = map.get(g);
   00326: 
   00327:     if (!entry) {
   00328:       map.set(g, {
   00329:         grupo: g,
   00330:         grupoNum: i,
   00331:         animal: official?.animal || "",
   00332:         slug: official?.slug || "",
   00333:         dezenas: official?.dezenas || [],
   00334:         total: 0,
   00335:       });
   00336:     } else {
   00337:       if (!entry.grupoNum) entry.grupoNum = i;
   00338:       if (!entry.animal) entry.animal = official?.animal || "";
   00339:       if (!entry.slug) entry.slug = official?.slug || "";
   00340:       if ((!entry.dezenas || !entry.dezenas.length) && official?.dezenas?.length) {
   00341:         entry.dezenas = official.dezenas;
   00342:       }
   00343:     }
   00344:   }
   00345: 
>> 00346:   const ranking = Array.from(map.values()).sort((a, b) => {
   00347:     const dt = Number(b.total) - Number(a.total);
   00348:     if (dt !== 0) return dt;
   00349:     return String(a.grupo).localeCompare(String(b.grupo));
   00350:   });
   00351: 
   00352:   return {
   00353:     ranking,
   00354:     top3: ranking.slice(0, 3),
   00355:     totalOcorrencias,
   00356:     integrityIssues,
>> 00357:     totalDraws,
>> 00358:     uniqueDays,
>> 00359:     minDate,
>> 00360:     maxDate,
   00361: 
   00362:     // útil para a próxima etapa (definição de regra)
   00363:     countMode,
   00364:   };
   00365: }

   00001: // src/utils/userIdentity.js
   00002: 
   00003: export function normalizeDigitsOnly(v) {
   00004:   return String(v ?? "").replace(/\D+/g, "");
   00005: }
   00006: 
   00007: export function normalizeCpfDigits(cpf) {
   00008:   return normalizeDigitsOnly(cpf).slice(0, 11);
   00009: }
   00010: 
   00011: /**
   00012:  * SHA-256 -> hex (robusto)
   00013:  * - tenta WebCrypto (window.crypto.subtle)
   00014:  * - fallback: CryptoJS (npm i crypto-js)
   00015:  */
   00016: async function sha256Hex(str) {
   00017:   const input = String(str || "");
   00018: 
   00019:   // 1) WebCrypto (preferido)
   00020:   try {
   00021:     const subtle = globalThis?.crypto?.subtle;
   00022:     if (subtle) {
   00023:       const enc = new TextEncoder().encode(input);
   00024:       const buf = await subtle.digest("SHA-256", enc);
>> 00025:       const arr = Array.from(new Uint8Array(buf));
   00026:       return arr.map((b) => b.toString(16).padStart(2, "0")).join("");
   00027:     }
   00028:   } catch {
   00029:     // cai pro fallback abaixo
   00030:   }
   00031: 
   00032:   // 2) Fallback via CryptoJS
   00033:   // Requer: npm i crypto-js
   00034:   const { default: CryptoJS } = await import("crypto-js");
   00035:   return CryptoJS.SHA256(input).toString(CryptoJS.enc.Hex);
   00036: }
   00037: 
   00038: /**
   00039:  * userId determinístico e não sensível
   00040:  * Ex: "cpf:ab12cd..."
   00041:  */
   00042: export async function userIdFromCpfDigits(cpfDigits11) {
   00043:   const cpf = String(cpfDigits11 || "").trim();
   00044:   if (cpf.length !== 11) return null;
   00045: 
   00046:   const h = await sha256Hex(cpf);
   00047:   return `cpf:${h}`;
   00048: }
   00049:
